data-v-a80ba730><h3 data-v-a80ba730>Secondary (41)</h3><p data-v-a80ba730> In addition to my main projects, I have a collection of secondary endeavors during my leisure time. These projects, though still a work in progress, serve as a wellspring of inspiration and creative exploration. These undertakings, though not adorned with the metaphorical red carpet treatment that my main projects receive, hold a special place in my coding repository.
